    This would put the company on track to meet its 2026 target

Stepping up drilling
    Looking ahead, Vista is considering adding another drilling rig

to its fleet next year to increase production by taking advantage
of a tax incentive program, Galuccio said. The company has
applied for benefits, including 30 years of tax breaks and legal
stability, for a project in its Bandurria Norte block in Vaca Muerta.

    This would allow it to drill more wells, with a current target of
100 to 110 new wells in 2027, or potentially more, Galuccio said.

    If the tax incentives are approved, Vista will bring forward its
development of Bandurria Norte to 2027 from 2029, he added.

    Vaca Muerta, in northern Patagonia, is one of the world's
largest shale plays. It is expected to drive a rise in Argentina's
total oil output to 2.2 million b/d by 2035 from 932,000 b/d in
August, while gas output is seen increasing to 290 million cu
m/d from 150 million cu m/d over the same period, according to
Buenos Aires-based consulting firm Aleph Energy.

                                                                -- Charles Newbery

Patterson-UTI says drilling recovery is tightening
US fracking capacity

US fracking capacity faces supply bottleneck

Diesel equipment retirements accelerate

Completion pricing recovers in Q3

    US hydraulic fracturing capacity could become a bottleneck
as rising drilling activity increases demand for well completions,
while equipment retirements, lengthy manufacturing lead
times and restrained investment limit the industry's response,
Patterson-UTI Energy Inc. executives said Oct. 29.

    "The completion demand is going to increase, but it's not clear
yet where the supply is going to come from," CEO Andy Hendricks
said on the company's third-quarter earnings call.

    The US shale industry has added more than 50 drilling rigs
since activity bottomed in the spring, but completion demand
has yet to fully reflect those additions, Hendricks said. That
demand is expected to rise over the next several quarters, just as
higher diesel costs accelerate the retirement of older pumping
equipment.

    Patterson-UTI plans to keep its overall completion capacity
relatively steady in 2027, replacing older diesel equipment with
natural gas-powered assets rather than expanding its fleet.
Hendricks said pricing and returns must improve further before
additional capacity becomes attractive, with stronger contracts
and visibility beyond a single year also needed to support
investment.

Gas-powered equipment tightens
    Legacy diesel equipment represents around 20% of active US

completion horsepower, according to Patterson-UTI's estimates
shared during the call. Higher diesel prices are making some of

of each year, which is when the oil seed and sugarcane harvest                           domestic supplies give Asia's top middle-distillate supplier greater

Agricultural demand makes diesel Brazil's top refined product in
terms of consumption, according to the ANP.

    Brazil also is evaluating higher biofuel-blending mandates
to reduce the need for imported barrels of diesel and gasoline.
Brazil currently blends 15% biodiesel with diesel sold at the
pump, with plans to increase the biodiesel mandate to 16% in
2027, according to the Mines and Energy Ministry. That should
help reduce diesel imports by about 2.4 billion liters.

    Each 1% increase in the volume of biodiesel blended with
diesel sold at the pump also represents about 600 million liters/
year of additional biodiesel production, according to Brazilian
Biodiesel and Biokerosene Union, or Ubrabio, and the Brazilian
Vegetable Oil Industry Association, or Abiove. Ubrabio and Abiove
are both trade groups that represent biodiesel producers.

                                                NWE Ports Eur/CBM Wkly                    · Type: Volume Unit
                                                                                          · Definition: A unit of volume that measures the volume of pure
To:                                                                                       (100%) alcohol. One HLTPA equals 1 hectoliter (100 liters) of pure
                                                                                          alcohol. Unlike a standard hectoliter (hL), which measures the
MDC Symbol Bates Dec Freq Curr UOM Description                                            total volume of any liquid, HLTPA refers only to the pure alcohol
                                                                                          content, regardless of the concentration in the original beverage.
